# Agents · T · part 14

1,041 entries, test-runner to test-writer. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **test-runner**: Test suite runner. Use PROACTIVELY to run the full pytest suite (or a subset) whenever tests need to be executed — after implementation changes, before commits, or on request. Keeps verbose test output out of the main context and reports only failures. ([raw-asparagus/gnn-nucleosynthesis](https://github.com/raw-asparagus/gnn-nucleosynthesis/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/test-runner.md))
- **test-runner**: Runs the pytest suite under uv and reports failures with the minimal diagnosis, keeping long test output out of the main conversation. Use after a code change, or when asked whether tests pass. Knows the slow-marker convention and the gamma-side branch split. ([raw-asparagus/hi-x-ugrb](https://github.com/raw-asparagus/hi-x-ugrb/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/test-runner.md))
- **test-runner**: Runs the test/lint suite for this repo (currently `bash -n` syntax checks on every hook script and JSON validation on settings) and reports pass/fail with concise output. Use after edits to .claude/hooks/, .claude/settings.json, or before committing. ([rayhansyahrizal/claude-agent-dev-kit](https://github.com/rayhansyahrizal/claude-agent-dev-kit/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/test-runner.md))
- **test-runner**: Runs a given test or check command and reports failures only. Use for every test run so verbose output never lands in the main context. ([rezarajan/datascape](https://github.com/rezarajan/datascape/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/test-runner.md))
- **test-runner**: Run test suites, checks, lint, and verification scripts, then report results compactly -- pass/fail counts, the exact failing assertions, the relevant output tail, and the path of the full log. Use whenever the main loop needs verification evidence without burning context on full test output. ([rizonesoft/Office](https://github.com/rizonesoft/Office/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/test-runner.md))
- **test-runner**: Runs the project's validation gates (build, lint, typecheck, tests, coverage, e2e, security) defined in .claude/gates.json and reports a clean pass/fail summary. - **test-runner**: Runs the full test suite (frontend build + unit tests + playwright browser checks) after a batch of issues has been implemented. Detects regressions — things that were working before but broke after the latest changes. Used by issue-dispatcher after each implementation batch. Examples: ([rogeriosantos/app-forge-teams](https://github.com/rogeriosantos/app-forge-teams/blob/HEAD/agents/test-runner.md))
- **test-runner**: Runs pytest for distl (full suite or a scoped subset) and reports results factually.
